workflows/deploy.yml =====
name: Deploy to Cloudflare Pages

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  build-deploy:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: read
      deployments: write

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v7

      - name: Setup Node
        uses: actions/setup-node@v7
        with:
          node-version: 24
          cache: npm

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: npm ci

      - name: Build
        run: npm run build

      - name: Deploy to Cloudflare Pages
        uses: cloudflare/wrangler-action@v4
        with:
          apiToken: ${{ secrets.CF_API_TOKEN }}
          accountId: ${{ secrets.CF_ACCOUNT_ID }}
          command: pages deploy dist --project-name=qimochi --branch=main

===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: ./.github/workflows/dumpl.yml =====
name: Dump Repo

on:
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  dump:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Build dump
        run: |
          OUTPUT="dump.txt"
          {
            echo "=== REPO DUMP ==="
            echo "Generated: $(date -u)"
            echo "Commit: $(git rev-parse --short HEAD)"
            echo ""

            echo "=== STRUCTURE ==="
            find . -type d \
              -not -path "./.git*" \
              -not -path "./node_modules*" \
              -not -path "./dist*" \
              -not -path "./.wrangler*" \
              -not -path "./.astro*" \
              -not -path "./.vercel*" \
              -not -path "./.netlify*" \
              | sort
            echo ""

            echo "=== FILES ==="
            find . -type f \
              -not -path "./.git/*" \
              -not -path "./node_modules/*" \
              -not -path "./dist/*" \
              -not -path "./.wrangler/*" \
              -not -path "./.astro/*" \
              -not -path "./.vercel/*" \
              -not -path "./.netlify/*" \
              -not -name "dump.txt" \
              -not -name "package-lock.json" \
              -not -name "pnpm-lock.yaml" \
              -not -name "yarn.lock" \
              -not -name "bun.lockb" \
              -not -name "*.log" \
              -not -name ".DS_Store" \
              -not -name "*.png" \
              -not -name "*.jpg" \
              -not -name "*.jpeg" \
              -not -name "*.gif" \
              -not -name "*.ico" \
              -not -name "*.svg" \
              -not -name "*.woff" \
              -not -name "*.woff2" \
              -not -name "*.ttf" \
              -not -name "*.otf" \
              -not -name "*.webp" \
              -not -name "*.avif" \
              -not -name "*.mp4" \
              -not -name "*.webm" \
              -not -name "*.pdf" \
              -not -name "*.zip" \
              -not -name "*.tar" \
              -not -name "*.gz" \
              -size -100k \
              | sort
            echo ""

            echo "=== CONTENTS ==="
            find . -type f \
              -not -path "./.git/*" \
              -not -path "./node_modules/*" \
              -not -path "./dist/*" \
              -not -path "./.wrangler/*" \
              -not -path "./.astro/*" \
              -not -path "./.vercel/*" \
              -not -path "./.netlify/*" \
              -not -name "dump.txt" \
              -not -name "package-lock.json" \
              -not -name "pnpm-lock.yaml" \
              -not -name "yarn.lock" \
              -not -name "bun.lockb" \
              -not -name "*.log" \
              -not -name ".DS_Store" \
              -not -name "*.png" \
              -not -name "*.jpg" \
              -not -name "*.jpeg" \
              -not -name "*.gif" \
              -not -name "*.ico" \
              -not -name "*.svg" \
              -not -name "*.woff" \
              -not -name "*.woff2" \
              -not -name "*.ttf" \
              -not -name "*.otf" \
              -not -name "*.webp" \
              -not -name "*.avif" \
              -not -name "*.mp4" \
              -not -name "*.webm" \
              -not -name "*.pdf" \
              -not -name "*.zip" \
              -not -name "*.tar" \
              -not -name "*.gz" \
              -size -100k \
              | sort | while read -r file; do
                echo "" >> "$OUTPUT"
                echo "===== FILE: $file =====" >> "$OUTPUT"
                sed -E \
                  -e 's/[0-9]{8,10}:[A-Za-z0-9_-]{35}/***TELEGRAM_TOKEN***/g' \
                  -e 's/(BOT_TOKEN|API_TOKEN|CLIENT_SECRET|API_KEY|PASSWORD|CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN|GITHUB_TOKEN|GH_TOKEN)[[:space:]]*=[[:space:]]*["'"'"']?[^"'"'"'[:space:]]+/\1=***REDACTED***/g' \
                  "$file" >> "$OUTPUT" 2>/dev/null || echo "[binary]" >> "$OUTPUT"
                echo "" >> "$OUTPUT"
                echo "===== END FILE =====" >> "$OUTPUT"
              done

            echo ""
            echo "=== DUMP COMPLETE ==="
          } > "$OUTPUT"

          echo "Generated: $(wc -l < $OUTPUT) lines, $(du -h $OUTPUT | cut -f1)"

      - name: Commit
        run: |
          git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
          git config user.email "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"
          git add dump.txt
          if git diff --staged --quiet; then
            echo "No changes."
          else
            git commit -m "chore: dump repo [skip ci]"
            git push
          fi
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: ./astro.config.mjs =====
import { defineConfig, fontProviders, svgoOptimizer } from 'astro/config';
import sitemap from '@astrojs/sitemap';

export default defineConfig({
  site: 'https://qimochi.pages.dev',
  trailingSlash: 'always',
  fonts: [
    {
      provider: fontProviders.local(),
      name: "Montserrat",
      cssVariable: "--font-montserrat",
      options: {
        variants: [
          {
            weight: "100 900",
            style: "normal",
            src: ["./src/assets/fonts/Montserrat.woff2"],
          },
        ],
      },
    },
    {
      provider: fontProviders.local(),
      name: "Inter",
      cssVariable: "--font-inter",
      options: {
        variants: [
          {
            weight: "100 900",
            style: "normal",
            src: ["./src/assets/fonts/Inter.woff2"],
          },
        ],
      },
    },
    {
      provider: fontProviders.local(),
      name: "FiraCode",
      cssVariable: "--font-firaCode",
      options: {
        variants: [
          {
            weight: "100 900",
            style: "normal",
            src: ["./src/assets/fonts/FiraCode.woff2"],
          },
        ],
      },
    },
  ],
  integrations: [
    sitemap({
      filter: (page) => {
        if (page.includes('/search/')) return false;
        if (page.includes('/404/')) return false;
        if (page.includes('/dmca/')) return false;
        if (page.includes('/anime/watch/')) return false;
        if (page.includes('/disclaimer/')) return false;
        if (/\/\d+\/$/.test(page)) return false;

        return true;
      },
      changefreq: 'daily',
      priority: 0.7,
      lastmod: new Date(),
    }),
  ],
  experimental: {
    incrementalBuild: true,
    svgOptimizer: svgoOptimizer(),
  },
});

===== FILE: ./src/components/ActionBar.astro =====
---
import { URLS } from '~/constants';
import LinkButton from '~/user-components/LinkButton.astro';

interface Props {
  slug?: string;
  episodeNumber?: number;
  totalEpisodes?: number;
}

const {
  slug = '',
  episodeNumber = 1,
  totalEpisodes = 0,
} = Astro.props;

const hasPrev = episodeNumber > 1;
const hasNext = episodeNumber < totalEpisodes;
const prevEp = hasPrev ? episodeNumber - 1 : null;
const nextEp = hasNext ? episodeNumber + 1 : null;
---

<nav class="action-bar" aria-label="Navigasi episode">
  {hasPrev ? (
    <LinkButton
      href={URLS.watch(slug, prevEp!)}
      variant="outline"
      size="md"
      icon="arrow-left"
    >
      Ep {prevEp}
    </LinkButton>
  ) : (
    <LinkButton
      variant="outline"
      size="md"
      icon="arrow-left"
      disabled
    >
      Ep —
    </LinkButton>
  )}

  {hasNext ? (
    <LinkButton
      href={URLS.watch(slug, nextEp!)}
      variant="outline"
      size="md"
      icon="arrow-right"
      iconPos="suffix"
    >
      Ep {nextEp}
    </LinkButton>
  ) : (
    <LinkButton
      variant="outline"
      size="md"
      icon="arrow-right"
      iconPos="suffix"
      disabled
    >
      Ep —
    </LinkButton>
  )}
</nav>

<style>
  .action-bar {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: var(--s-2);
  }
</style>
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: ./src/components/AnimeCard.astro =====
---
import Badge from '~/user-components/Badge.astro';
import { URLS, STATUS_VARIANT, type Status } from '~/constants';

interface Props {
  anime: {
    id: string;
    data: {
      title: string;
      cover: string;
      status: string;
      type: string;
      releaseDate: Date;
      episodes?: { number: number }[];
    };
  };
  featured?: boolean;
  relationBadge?: string;
}

const {
  anime,
  featured = false,
  relationBadge,
} = Astro.props;

const { id, data } = anime;

const variant = STATUS_VARIANT[data.status as Status] ?? 'default';

const lastEpisode = data.episodes?.length
  ? Math.max(...data.episodes.map(e => e.number))
  : 0;
---

<a
  data-anime-title={data.title}
  href={URLS.anime(id)}
  class:list={['anime-card', { 'anime-card-featured': featured }]}
>
  <div class="cover">
    <img
      src={data.cover}
      alt={`Cover ${data.title}`}
      width="300"
      height="450"
      loading="lazy"
      decoding="async"
    />

    <span class="type-badge">{data.type}</span>

    <div class="status-overlay">
      <Badge variant={variant} size="sm">{data.status}</Badge>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="info">
    <h3 set:html={data.title} />

    {relationBadge && (
      <span class="relation-badge">{relationBadge}</span>
    )}

    {lastEpisode > 0 && !relationBadge && (
      <span class="episodes">Ep {lastEpisode}</span>
    )}

    {lastEpisode > 0 && relationBadge && (
      <span class="episodes">
        {relationBadge} · Ep {lastEpisode}
      </span>
    )}
  </div>
</a>

<style>
  .anime-card {
    display: block;
    min-width: 0;
    background: var(--bg-elevated);
    border: 1px solid transparent;
    overflow: hidden;
    text-decoration: none;
    color: inherit;
    transition: transform var(--t-fast);
  }

  .cover {
    position: relative;
    aspect-ratio: 2 / 3;
    overflow: hidden;
    background: var(--bg-surface);
  }

  .cover img {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    object-fit: cover;
    display: block;
    transition: transform var(--t-base);
  }

  .anime-card:hover .cover img {
    transform: scale(1.03);
  }

  .anime-card:hover h3::after {
    opacity: 1;
  }

  .cover::after {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    background: linear-gradient(
      to bottom,
      transparent 50%,
      rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6) 100%
    );
    pointer-events: none;
  }

  .type-badge {
    position: absolute;
    top: var(--s-2);
    right: var(--s-2);
    z-index: 2;
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;

    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    text-transform: uppercase;

  }

  .status-overlay {
    position: absolute;
    bottom: var(--s-2);

    & span {
      text-transform: uppercase;
    }
  }

  .info {
    padding: var(--s-3);
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;

    min-width: 0;
  }

  .info h3 {
    position: relative;

===== FILE: ./src/components/AnimeFranchise.astro =====
---
import AnimeCard from './AnimeCard.astro';
import { getAllAnime } from '~/lib/anime';

const RELATION_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  sequel: 'Sekuel',
  prequel: 'Prekuel',
  side_story: 'Cerita Sampingan',
  parent_story: 'Cerita Induk',
  alternative: 'Versi Alternatif',
  spin_off: 'Spin-off',
  adaptation: 'Adaptasi',
  character: 'Karakter',
  summary: 'Ringkasan',
  full_story: 'Cerita Lengkap',
  compilation: 'Kompilasi',
  contains: 'Berisi',
  other: 'Terkait',
};

interface Franchise {
  relation: string;
  slug: string;
  title?: string;
}

interface Props {
  title?: string;
  currentSlug: string;
  franchises?: Franchise[];
}

const {
  title = 'Franchise',
  currentSlug,
  franchises = [],
} = Astro.props;

const filtered = franchises.filter((f) => f.slug !== currentSlug);

if (filtered.length === 0) return null;

const allAnime = await getAllAnime();
const animeMap = new Map(allAnime.map((a) => [a.id, a]));

function labelFor(relation: string): string {
  return RELATION_LABEL[relation] ?? relation;
}

function slugToTitle(slug: string): string {
  return slug
    .split('-')
    .map((w) => w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1))
    .join(' ');
}
---

<section class="anime-franchise">
  <h2 class="anime-franchise__title">{title}</h2>

  <div class="anime-franchise__grid">
    {filtered.map((f) => {
      const anime = animeMap.get(f.slug);
      const label = labelFor(f.relation);

      if (anime) {
        return (
          <AnimeCard
            anime={anime}
            relationBadge={label}
          />
        );
      }

      const displayTitle = f.title ?? slugToTitle(f.slug);

      return (
        <div class="anime-franchise__dummy" aria-disabled="true">
          <div class="anime-franchise__dummy-cover">
            <span class="anime-franchise__dummy-label">{label}</span>
          </div>
          <p class="anime-franchise__dummy-title">{displayTitle}</p>
          <span class="anime-franchise__dummy-meta">Belum tersedia</span>
        </div>
      );
    })}
  </div>
</section>

<details class="episode-list" open>
  <summary>
    <span class="label">Episode</span>
    <span class="count">({episodes.length})</span>
    <span class="chevron" aria-hidden="true">
      <Icon name="chevron-down" size={14} />
    </span>
  </summary>

            href={URLS.watch(slug, num)}
            class:list={['episode-item', { active: isActive }]}
            aria-current={isActive ? 'page' : undefined}
          >
            <span class="num">{String(num).padStart(2, '0')}</span>
          </a>
        </li>
      );
    })}
  </ol>

===== FILE: ./src/components/Head.astro =====
---
import { SITE } from '~/constants';
import { Font } from "astro:assets";

interface Props {
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  image?: string;
  canonical?: string;
  noindex?: boolean;
  type?: 'website' | 'article' | 'video.other';
  publishedTime?: string;
}

const {
  title,
  description = SITE.description,
  image,
  canonical,
  noindex = false,
  type = 'website',
  publishedTime,
} = Astro.props;

const canonicalURL = canonical
  ? new URL(canonical, Astro.site)
  : new URL(Astro.url.pathname, Astro.site);

const fullTitle = title === SITE.name ? title : `${title} — ${SITE.name}`;

const ogImage = image
  ? new URL(image, Astro.site)
  : new URL('/images/og-default.png', Astro.site);

const twitterCard = image ? 'summary_large_image' : 'summary';
---

<meta charset="UTF-8" />
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />

<title>{fullTitle}</title>
<meta name="description" content={description} />
<link rel="canonical" href={canonicalURL} />

{noindex ? (
  <meta name="robots" content="noindex, follow" />
) : (
  <meta name="robots" content="index, follow" />
)}

<meta property="og:type" content={type} />
<meta property="og:title" content={fullTitle} />
<meta property="og:description" content={description} />
<meta property="og:url" content={canonicalURL} />
<meta property="og:image" content={ogImage} />
<meta property="og:site_name" content={SITE.name} />
<meta property="og:locale" content={SITE.locale} />
{publishedTime && (
  <meta property="article:published_time" content={publishedTime} />
)}

<meta name="twitter:card" content={twitterCard} />
<meta name="twitter:title" content={fullTitle} />
<meta name="twitter:description" content={description} />
<meta name="twitter:image" content={ogImage} />

<meta name="theme-color" content="#0a0a0f" />
<meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-title" content={SITE.name} />

<link rel="icon" type="image/png" href="/images/favicon/favicon-96x96.png" sizes="96x96" />
<link rel="icon" type="image/svg+xml" href="/images/favicon/favicon.svg" />
<link rel="shortcut icon" href="/images/favicon/favicon.ico" />
<link rel="apple-touch-icon" sizes="180x180" href="/images/favicon/apple-touch-icon.png" />
<link rel="manifest" href="/images/favicon/site.webmanifest" />

<link rel="sitemap" href="/sitemap-index.xml" />
<link
  rel="alternate"
  type="application/rss+xml"
  title={`${SITE.name} — Blog`}
  href="/rss.xml"
/>

<Font cssVariable="--font-montserrat" preload />
<Font cssVariable="--font-inter" preload />
<Font cssVariable="--font-firaCode" preload />

===== FILE: ./src/components/Header.astro =====
---
import { SITE, NAV_LINKS } from '~/constants';
import { Image } from 'astro:assets';
import logoImg from '~/assets/images/logo/logo.png';
import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';

const currentPath = Astro.url.pathname;

function isActive(href: string): boolean {
  if (href === '/') return currentPath === '/';
  const matches = currentPath === href || currentPath.startsWith(href);
  if (!matches) return false;
  const hasMoreSpecific = NAV_LINKS.some(({ href: otherHref }) => {
    if (otherHref === href) return false;
    if (otherHref === '/') return false;
    if (otherHref.length <= href.length) return false;
    return currentPath === otherHref || currentPath.startsWith(otherHref);
  });
  return !hasMoreSpecific;
}
---

<header class="site-header">
  <div class="header-inner">
    <a href="/" class="logo" aria-label={`${SITE.name} — Home`}>
      <Image
        src={logoImg}
        alt={SITE.name}
        width={64}
        height={64}
        loading="eager"
        fetchpriority="high"
        decoding="async"
        class="logo-img"
      />
    </a>

    <div class="header-right">

          <span class="icon-close"><Icon name="close" size={22} /></span>

<script>
  const menu = document.querySelector<HTMLDetailsElement>('.menu');
  if (menu) {
    const mobile = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 50rem)');

    if (mobile.matches) menu.removeAttribute('open');

    mobile.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      if (e.matches) menu.removeAttribute('open');
      else menu.setAttribute('open', '');
    });

    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (!mobile.matches) return;
      if (!menu.hasAttribute('open')) return;
      if (!menu.contains(e.target as Node)) menu.removeAttribute('open');
    });

    menu.querySelectorAll('.nav a').forEach((a) => {
      a.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (mobile.matches) menu.removeAttribute('open');
      });
    });

    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && mobile.matches && menu.hasAttribute('open')) {
        menu.removeAttribute('open');
      }
    });
  }
</script>

===== FILE: ./src/components/Pagination.astro =====
---
import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';

interface Props {
  current: number;
  total: number;
  base: string;
}

const { current, total, base } = Astro.props;

if (total <= 1) return null;

function urlFor(page: number): string {
  return page === 1 ? `${base}/` : `${base}/${page}/`;
}

function buildPages(): (number | '...')[] {
  if (total <= 7) {
    return Array.from({ length: total }, (_, i) => i + 1);
  }

  const pages: (number | '...')[] = [1];

  if (current > 3) pages.push('...');

  const start = Math.max(2, current - 1);
  const end = Math.min(total - 1, current + 1);

  for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) pages.push(i);

  if (current < total - 2) pages.push('...');
  pages.push(total);

  return pages;
}

const pages = buildPages();
---

<nav class="pagination" aria-label="Navigasi halaman">
  {current > 1 ? (
    <a href={urlFor(current - 1)} class="page-btn" rel="prev" aria-label="Halaman sebelumnya">
      <Icon name="arrow-left" />
    </a>
  ) : (
    <span class="page-btn disabled" aria-hidden="true">
      <Icon name="arrow-left" />
    </span>
  )}

  {pages.map(p =>
    p === '...' ? (
      <span class="page-ellipsis">…</span>
    ) : p === current ? (
      <span class="page-num active" aria-current="page">{p}</span>
    ) : (
      <a href={urlFor(p)} class="page-num">{p}</a>
    )
  )}

  {current < total ? (
    <a href={urlFor(current + 1)} class="page-btn" rel="next" aria-label="Halaman berikutnya">
      <Icon name="arrow-right" />
    </a>
  ) : (
    <span class="page-btn disabled" aria-hidden="true">
      <Icon name="arrow-right" />
    </span>
  )}
</nav>

===== FILE: ./src/components/StreamSelector.astro =====
---
import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';

interface Server {
  name: string;
  url: string;
}
interface Stream {
  quality: string;
  servers: Server[];
}

interface Props {
  streams?: Stream[];
}

const { streams = [] } = Astro.props;
const defaultStream = streams[0];
const defaultServer = defaultStream?.servers?.[0];
---

<details class="stream-picker" open>
  <summary>
    <span class="label">Server &amp; Kualitas</span>
    <span class="current" data-current>
      {defaultStream ? `${defaultStream.quality} · ${defaultServer?.name ?? '—'}` : '—'}
    </span>
    <span class="chevron" aria-hidden="true">
      <Icon name="chevron-down" size={16} />
    </span>
  </summary>

  <div class="stream-body">
    {streams.map((s, i) => (
      <div class="quality-group">
        <span class="quality-label">{s.quality}</span>
        <div class="chips">
          {s.servers.map((srv, j) => {
            const id = `s-${i}-${j}`;
            const isDefault = i === 0 && j === 0;
            return (
              <>
                <input
                  type="radio"
                  name="stream"
                  id={id}
                  value={srv.url}
                  data-quality={s.quality}
                  data-server={srv.name}
                  checked={isDefault}
                  hidden
                />
                <label for={id} class="chip">{srv.name}</label>
              </>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    ))}
  </div>
</details>

<script>
  // ------------------------------------------------------------
  // Ganti server: update iframe src + label + simpan preferensi
  // ------------------------------------------------------------
  const picker = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.stream-picker');
  const iframe = document.getElementById('player-iframe') as HTMLIFrameElement | null;
  const currentLabel = picker?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-current]') ?? null;

  if (picker && iframe) {
    // Restore saved preference (kalau ada)
    const savedId = localStorage.getItem('qimochi:stream');
    if (savedId) {
      const savedInput = document.getElementById(savedId) as HTMLInputElement | null;
      if (savedInput && savedInput.name === 'stream') {
        savedInput.checked = true;
        iframe.src = savedInput.value;
        if (currentLabel) {
          const q = savedInput.dataset.quality ?? '';
          const s = savedInput.dataset.server ?? '';
          currentLabel.textContent = `${q} · ${s}`;
        }
      }
    }

    // Listen perubahan radio
    picker.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      const target = e.target as HTMLInputElement;
      if (!target.matches('input[name="stream"]')) return;
      if (!target.checked) return;

      iframe.src = target.value;

      if (currentLabel) {
        const q = target.dataset.quality ?? '';
        const s = target.dataset.server ?? '';
        currentLabel.textContent = `${q} · ${s}`;
      }

      localStorage.setItem('qimochi:stream', target.id);
    });
  }
</script>

  'Hiatus':    'warning',

  preferredServer:  'qimochi:preferred-server',
} as const;

export const URLS = {
  anime:  (slug: string) => `/anime/${slug}/`,
  watch: (slug, episode) => `/anime/watch/${slug}/episodes/${episode}/`,
  genre:  (slug: string) => `/anime/genre/${slug.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '-')}/`,
  status: (status: string) => `/anime/${status.toLowerCase()}/`,
} as const;

===== FILE: ./src/content.config.ts =====
// src/content.config.ts
import { defineCollection } from 'astro:content';
import { glob } from 'astro/loaders';
import { z } from 'astro/zod';

const anime = defineCollection({
  loader: glob({ pattern: '**/*.md', base: './src/content/anime' }),
  schema: z.object({
    title: z.string(),
    cover: z.url(),
    status: z.enum(['Ongoing', 'Completed', 'Hiatus']),
    type: z.enum(['TV', 'Movie', 'OVA', 'ONA', 'Special']),
    genre: z.array(z.string()).min(1),
    studio: z.string(),
    releaseDate: z.coerce.date(),
    addedAt: z.coerce.date(),
    rating: z.number().min(0).max(10),
  }),
});

const blog = defineCollection({
  loader: glob({ pattern: '**/*.md', base: './src/content/blog' }),
  schema: z.object({
    title: z.string().max(120),
    description: z.string().max(200),
    cover: z.url().optional(),
    date: z.coerce.date(),
    updatedAt: z.coerce.date().optional(),
    author: z.string().default('Admin'),
    category: z.enum(['News', 'Review', 'List', 'Guide', 'Update']),
    tags: z.array(z.string()).default([]),
    draft: z.boolean().default(false),
  }),
});

export const collections = { anime, blog };
===== END FILE =====

studio: "Brain's Base"
releaseDate: 2026-04-11
addedAt: 2026-10-06
rating: 6.4
---

Hiyori Hiwamura, seorang guru kelas di sekolah menengah, dikenal sebagai “Guru Menakutkan” oleh para siswanya. Banyak yang percaya bahwa jika mereka melakukan kesalahan, Hiyori akan mengutuk mereka. Namun, di balik reputasinya yang menakutkan, Hiyori sebenarnya memiliki hati yang lembut dan sering kali disalahpahami.

Sebagai guru kelas, Hiyori memiliki cara yang unik dalam menghadapi siswanya. Meskipun banyak yang takut padanya, ada satu siswa yang secara tidak sengaja menemukan sisi lain dari Hiyori. Akihito Abikura, siswa yang cerdas dan penasaran, telah mengetahui rahasia yang disembunyikan Hiyori selama ini.

Dengan penemuan ini, Akihito sekarang memiliki kesempatan untuk melihat sisi lain dari Hiyori, yang mungkin akan mengubah persepsi dirinya tentang gurunya. Apakah Akihito akan dapat memahami Hiyori dengan lebih baik, ataukah rahasia ini akan membawa konsekuensi yang tidak terduga?

===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: ./src/content/anime/yozakura-san-chi-no-daisakusen-2nd-season.md =====
---
title: Yozakura-san Chi no Daisakusen 2nd Season
cover: https://s4.anilist.co/file/anilistcdn/media/anime/cover/large/bx182578-Te8W0EwesgrS.jpg
status: Completed
type: TV
genre: [Action, Comedy, Romance]
studio: SILVER LINK.
releaseDate: 2026-04-12
addedAt: 2026-10-06
rating: 7.5
---

Taiyou Asano telah menetap dalam karirnya sebagai anggota keluarga mata-mata Yozakura setelah menikah dengan Mutsumi Yozakura. Meskipun dia masih berjuang untuk mengontrol kekuatan yang diberikan oleh istrinya, Taiyou bekerja keras untuk menyelesaikan misi bersama saudara-saudaranya yang baru. Setelah menghancurkan organisasi Tanpopo, Taiyou dan keluarga Yozakura setuju untuk merawat Ai, seorang anak berusia enam tahun yang merupakan salah satu subjek tes Tanpopo. Namun, kesehatan Taiyou mulai memburuk karena darah Yozakura dalam tubuhnya. Dia bermimpi bahwa pendiri pertama keluarga, Tsubomi Yozakura, memberinya misi baru: membunuhnya. Untuk itu, dia harus belajar lebih banyak tentang keluarganya yang baru, menjadi mata-mata peringkat perak, dan mendorong dirinya lebih keras untuk sukses dan terus melindungi keluarganya.

===== FILE: ./src/content/blog/selamat-datang-di-qimochi.md =====
---
title: "Selamat Datang di Qimochi — Situs Streaming & Download Anime Batch"
description: "Perkenalan lengkap tentang Qimochi — fitur, cara pakai, dan komitmen kami untuk pengalaman nonton anime yang ringan dan cepat."
cover: https://cdn.myanimelist.net/images/anime/1171/109222.jpg
date: 2026-02-15
updatedAt: 2026-02-25
author: Admin
category: Update
tags: [Announcement, Qimochi, Guide]
draft: false
---

Selamat datang di **Qimochi** — tempat untuk streaming dan download anime batch
dengan pengalaman yang ringan, cepat, dan tanpa ribet.

## Apa Itu Qimochi?

Qimochi adalah koleksi anime yang bisa kamu tonton langsung atau download
dalam berbagai kualitas. Kami fokus pada tiga hal utama:

### 1. Kecepatan

Situs dibangun dengan teknologi statis modern. Setiap halaman dimuat dalam
waktu kurang dari 1 detik, bahkan di koneksi lambat.

### 2. Kemudahan

Navigasi simpel — tidak ada popup, tidak ada iklan mengganggu, tidak ada
redirect aneh. Kamu datang untuk nonton, kami pastikan itu yang kamu dapat.

### 3. Kelengkapan

Koleksi anime terus bertambah. Dari judul klasik sampai musim terbaru,
semuanya ada di satu tempat.

## Fitur Utama

- **Streaming langsung** — tonton tanpa download
- **Download batch** — unduh semua episode sekaligus
- **Multi-kualitas** — 360p, 480p, 720p, 1080p
- **Multiple server** — ganti server kalau yang satu lag
- **Pencarian canggih** — cari dengan typo tetap ketemu
- **Archive lengkap** — browse berdasarkan abjad
- **Genre filter** — temukan sesuai selera

## Cara Menggunakan

### 1. Browse Anime

Buka halaman [Archive](/anime/) untuk melihat semua anime. Atau jelajahi
berdasarkan [genre](/genre/) kalau kamu punya preferensi tertentu.

### 2. Pilih Episode

Setiap anime punya halaman detail dengan daftar episode lengkap. Klik
episode yang mau ditonton.

### 3. Pilih Server & Kualitas

Di halaman tonton, kamu bisa memilih:

- **Kualitas** — dari 360p (hemat kuota) sampai 1080p (kualitas terbaik)
- **Server** — ganti-ganti kalau yang satu bermasalah

Pilihanmu akan **diingat otomatis** untuk kunjungan berikutnya.

### 4. Nonton atau Download

- **Nonton langsung** — klik server, video langsung main
- **Download** — pilih kualitas, klik server download

## Yang Perlu Diketahui

Kami **tidak menyimpan file video** di server kami. Semua konten di-embed
dari layanan pihak ketiga yang tersedia bebas di internet. Kami hanya
menyediakan tautan untuk keperluan referensi.

Untuk informasi lebih lanjut, lihat halaman [DMCA](/dmca/) dan
[Disclaimer](/disclaimer/).

## Bergabung dengan Komunitas

Punya pertanyaan, saran, atau menemukan bug? Kami selalu terbuka untuk
masukan. Sampaikan lewat:

- **RSS Feed** — subscribe di [RSS](/rss.xml) untuk update artikel baru
- **Komentar** — (segera hadir)

## Apa Selanjutnya?

Kami terus mengembangkan Qimochi. Beberapa fitur yang sedang dalam
perencanaan:

- Sistem komentar untuk diskusi
- Watchlist pribadi
- Notifikasi episode baru
- Rekomendasi personal

Pantau terus blog ini untuk update terbaru. Selamat menjelajah! 🎉
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: ./src/lib/anime.ts =====
// src/lib/anime.ts
import { getCollection, getEntry, type CollectionEntry } from 'astro:content';

export interface EpisodeServer {
  name: string;
  url: string;
}

export interface EpisodeStream {
  quality: string;
  servers: EpisodeServer[];
}

export interface EpisodeDownload {
  quality: string;
  size: string;
  servers: EpisodeServer[];
}

export interface EpisodeData {
  number: number;
  title?: string;
  streams: EpisodeStream[];
  downloads?: EpisodeDownload[];
}

export interface Franchise {
  relation: string;
  slug: string;
  title?: string;
}

export type AnimeEntry = CollectionEntry<'anime'>;

export type HydratedAnime = Omit<AnimeEntry, 'data'> & {
  data: AnimeEntry['data'] & {
    episodes: EpisodeData[];
    franchises: Franchise[];
  };
};

interface ChunkRef {
  slug: string;
  start: number;
  end: number;
  path: string;
}

const HIDDEN_RELATIONS = new Set([
  'character',
  'adaptation',
  'contains',
  'other',
]);

const episodeModules = import.meta.glob<{ default: EpisodeData[] }>(
  '../data/anime/*/episodes/*.json',
  { eager: true }
);

const franchiseModules = import.meta.glob<{ default: Franchise[] }>(
  '../data/anime/*/franchises.json',
  { eager: true }
);

const chunksBySlug: Record<string, ChunkRef[]> = {};

for (const [path, mod] of Object.entries(episodeModules)) {
  const match = path.match(
    /\/data\/anime\/([^/]+)\/episodes\/(\d+)-(\d+)\.json$/
  );

  if (!match) {
    console.warn(`[Anime] Unexpected episode path (skip): ${path}`);
    continue;
  }

  const slug = match[1];
  const start = parseInt(match[2] ?? '0', 10);
  const end = parseInt(match[3] ?? '0', 10);

  if (!slug || isNaN(start) || isNaN(end)) {
    console.warn(`[Anime] Invalid chunk range: ${path}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (!Array.isArray(mod.default)) {
    console.warn(`[Anime] Chunk is not an array: ${path}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (!chunksBySlug[slug]) chunksBySlug[slug] = [];
  chunksBySlug[slug]!.push({ slug, start, end, path });
}

const episodesBySlug: Record<string, EpisodeData[]> = {};

for (const [slug, chunks] of Object.entries(chunksBySlug)) {
  chunks.sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);

  const merged: EpisodeData[] = [];

  for (const chunk of chunks) {
    const mod = episodeModules[chunk.path];
    if (!mod || !Array.isArray(mod.default)) continue;
    merged.push(...mod.default);
  }

  merged.sort((a, b) => a.number - b.number);
  episodesBySlug[slug] = merged;
}

const franchisesBySlug: Record<string, Franchise[]> = {};

for (const [path, mod] of Object.entries(franchiseModules)) {
  const match = path.match(/\/data\/anime\/([^/]+)\/franchises\.json$/);
  if (!match) continue;

  const slug = match[1];
  if (!slug) continue;

  const raw = Array.isArray(mod.default) ? mod.default : [];
  const filtered = raw.filter(
    (f) => f && typeof f.slug === 'string' && !HIDDEN_RELATIONS.has(f.relation)
  );

  franchisesBySlug[slug] = filtered;
}

async function hydrateOne(anime: AnimeEntry): Promise<HydratedAnime> {
  return {
    ...anime,
    data: {
      ...anime.data,
      episodes: episodesBySlug[anime.id] ?? [],
      franchises: franchisesBySlug[anime.id] ?? [],
    },
  };
}

export async function getAllAnime(): Promise<HydratedAnime[]> {
  const all = await getCollection('anime');
  return Promise.all(all.map(hydrateOne));
}

export async function getAnimeById(
  id: string
): Promise<HydratedAnime | null> {
  const anime = await getEntry('anime', id);
  if (!anime) return null;
  return hydrateOne(anime);
}

export function sortByRecent(items: HydratedAnime[]): HydratedAnime[] {
  return [...items].sort(
    (a, b) => b.data.addedAt.getTime() - a.data.addedAt.getTime()
  );
}

export function getYear(anime: HydratedAnime | AnimeEntry): number {
  return anime.data.releaseDate.getFullYear();
}

export interface EpisodePathItem {
  slug: string;
  anime: HydratedAnime;
  episode: EpisodeData;
  episodeIndex: number;
  totalEpisodes: number;
}

export async function getAllEpisodePaths(): Promise<EpisodePathItem[]> {
  const all = await getAllAnime();
  const paths: EpisodePathItem[] = [];

  for (const anime of all) {
    const eps = anime.data.episodes;
    const total = eps.length;

    eps.forEach((episode, episodeIndex) => {
      paths.push({
        slug: anime.id,
        anime,
        episode,
        episodeIndex,
        totalEpisodes: total,
      });
    });
  }

  return paths;
}

export function findEpisode(
  anime: HydratedAnime,
  episodeNumber: number
): EpisodeData | null {
  return anime.data.episodes.find((e) => e.number === episodeNumber) ?? null;
}
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: ./src/lib/blog.ts =====
import { getCollection } from 'astro:content';
import type { CollectionEntry } from 'astro:content';

export type BlogEntry = CollectionEntry<'blog'>;

/**
 * Ambil semua artikel blog yang published (bukan draft).
 * Sort by date descending (terbaru dulu).
 */
export async function getPublishedPosts(): Promise<BlogEntry[]> {
  const allPosts = await getCollection('blog', ({ data }) => !data.draft);
  return [...allPosts].sort(
    (a, b) => b.data.date.getTime() - a.data.date.getTime()
  );
}

/**
 * Ambil artikel by slug — return null kalau draft atau tidak ada.
 */
export async function getPublishedPostBySlug(
  slug: string
): Promise<BlogEntry | null> {
  const allPosts = await getCollection('blog');
  const post = allPosts.find(p => p.id === slug);
  if (!post || post.data.draft) return null;
  return post;
}

/**
 * Ambil semua slug artikel published — untuk getStaticPaths.
 */
export async function getAllPostSlugs(): Promise<string[]> {
  const posts = await getPublishedPosts();
  return posts.map(p => p.id);
}

/**
 * Format tanggal ke format Indonesia.
 * Contoh: "15 Februari 2026"
 */
export function formatDate(date: Date): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(date);
}

/**
 * Format tanggal singkat.
 * Contoh: "15 Feb 2026"
 */
export function formatDateShort(date: Date): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(date);
}

/**
 * Ambil artikel terkait berdasarkan tag yang sama.
 * Return max `limit` artikel, exclude artikel itu sendiri.
 */
export function getRelatedPosts(
  current: BlogEntry,
  all: BlogEntry[],
  limit = 3
): BlogEntry[] {
  const currentTags = new Set(current.data.tags);

  if (currentTags.size === 0) return [];

  return all
    .filter(post => post.id !== current.id)
    .map(post => ({
      post,
      score: post.data.tags.filter(t => currentTags.has(t)).length,
    }))
    .filter(({ score }) => score > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score)
    .slice(0, limit)
    .map(({ post }) => post);
}

/**
 * Ambil semua tag unik dari artikel published + jumlah per tag.
 * Sort by count descending, lalu alfabetis.
 */
export function collectTags(
  posts: BlogEntry[]
): { tag: string; count: number }[] {
  const counts: Record<string, number> = {};

  posts.forEach(post => {
    post.data.tags.forEach(tag => {
      counts[tag] = (counts[tag] || 0) + 1;
    });
  });

  return Object.entries(counts)
    .map(([tag, count]) => ({ tag, count }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count || a.tag.localeCompare(b.tag, 'id-ID'));
}

/**
 * Ambil artikel by tag.
 */
export function filterByTag(posts: BlogEntry[], tag: string): BlogEntry[] {
  return posts.filter(post => post.data.tags.includes(tag));
}

/**
 * Ambil artikel by category.
 */
export function filterByCategory(
  posts: BlogEntry[],
  category: string
): BlogEntry[] {
  return posts.filter(post => post.data.category === category);
}

/**
 * Build canonical URL untuk artikel blog.
 */
export function postUrl(slug: string): string {
  return `/blog/${slug}/`;
}
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: ./src/lib/genre.ts =====
// src/lib/genre.ts
import { GENRES, PER_PAGE } from '../constants';
import {
  getAllAnime,
  sortByRecent,
  type HydratedAnime,
} from './anime';

export type { HydratedAnime };

export function genreToSlug(genre: string): string {
  return genre.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '-');
}

export async function getAnimeByGenre(
  genre: string
): Promise<HydratedAnime[]> {
  const allAnime = await getAllAnime();
  const filtered = allAnime.filter((a) => a.data.genre.includes(genre));
  return sortByRecent(filtered);
}

export async function getGenreData(genre: string) {
  const anime = await getAnimeByGenre(genre);
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(anime.length / PER_PAGE);
  return { anime, totalPages, slug: genreToSlug(genre) };
}

export function paginate<T>(items: T[], page: number): T[] {
  const start = (page - 1) * PER_PAGE;
  return items.slice(start, start + PER_PAGE);
}

export function allGenreSlugs() {
  return GENRES.map((g) => ({
    genre: g,
    slug: genreToSlug(g),
  }));
}
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: ./src/lib/status.ts =====
// src/lib/status.ts
import { PER_PAGE } from '../constants';
import {
  getAllAnime,
  sortByRecent,
  type HydratedAnime,
} from './anime';

export const STATUS_MAP = {
  ongoing: 'Ongoing',
  complete: 'Completed',
  hiatus: 'Hiatus',
} as const;

export type StatusSlug = keyof typeof STATUS_MAP;

export async function getAnimeByStatus(
  status: string
): Promise<HydratedAnime[]> {
  const allAnime = await getAllAnime();
  const filtered = allAnime.filter((a) => a.data.status === status);
  return sortByRecent(filtered);
}

export async function getStatusData(slug: StatusSlug) {
  const status = STATUS_MAP[slug];
  const anime = await getAnimeByStatus(status);
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(anime.length / PER_PAGE);
  return { status, anime, totalPages };
}

export function buildStatusPaths(
  slug: StatusSlug,
  anime: HydratedAnime[],
  totalPages: number
) {
  const paths = [
    {
      params: { page: undefined },
      props: { anime, totalPages, pageNumber: 1 },
    },
  ];

  for (let page = 2; page <= totalPages; page++) {
    paths.push({
      params: { page: String(page) },
      props: { anime, totalPages, pageNumber: page },
    });
  }

  return paths;
}

export function paginate<T>(items: T[], page: number): T[] {
  const start = (page - 1) * PER_PAGE;
  return items.slice(start, start + PER_PAGE);
}
===== END FILE =====

// src/pages/anime.json.ts
import type { APIRoute } from 'astro';
import { getAllAnime } from '~/lib/anime';

export const GET: APIRoute = async () => {
  const allAnime = await getAllAnime();

  const data = allAnime.map((a) => ({
    id: a.id,
    title: a.data.title,
    cover: a.data.cover,
    type: a.data.type,
    status: a.data.status,
    releaseDate: a.data.releaseDate.toISOString(),
    rating: a.data.rating,
    genre: a.data.genre,
    studio: a.data.studio,
    episodes: a.data.episodes.map((e) => ({ number: e.number })),
  }));

  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
  });
};

===== FILE: ./src/pages/anime/[slug].json.ts =====
// src/pages/anime/[slug].json.ts
import type { APIRoute } from 'astro';
import { getAllAnime } from '~/lib/anime';

export async function getStaticPaths() {
  const allAnime = await getAllAnime();
  return allAnime.map((anime) => ({
    params: { slug: anime.id },
  }));
}

export const GET: APIRoute = async ({ params }) => {
  const { slug } = params;
  if (!slug) {
    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify({ error: { code: 'BAD_REQUEST', message: 'Missing slug' } }),
      { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    );
  }

  const allAnime = await getAllAnime();
  const anime = allAnime.find((a) => a.id === slug);

  if (!anime) {
    return new Response(
      JSON.stringify({ error: { code: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'Anime not found' } }),
      { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
    );
  }

  const data = {
    id: anime.id,
    title: anime.data.title,
    cover: anime.data.cover,
    status: anime.data.status,
    type: anime.data.type,
    rating: anime.data.rating,
    releaseDate: anime.data.releaseDate,
    genres: anime.data.genre,
    studio: anime.data.studio,
    totalEpisodes: anime.data.episodes.length,
    episodes: anime.data.episodes,
  };

  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8',
      'Cache-Control': 'public, max-age=3600, s-maxage=86400',
    },
  });
};
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: ./src/pages/anime/genre/[slug]/[...page].astro =====
---
import { getGenreData, paginate, allGenreSlugs } from '~/lib/genre';

import BaseLayout from '~/layouts/BaseLayout.astro';
import Hero from '~/components/Hero.astro';
import AnimeCard from '~/components/AnimeCard.astro';
import Pagination from '~/components/Pagination.astro';

export async function getStaticPaths() {
  const paths = [];

  for (const { genre, slug } of allGenreSlugs()) {
    const { anime, totalPages } = await getGenreData(genre);

    if (anime.length === 0) continue;

    paths.push({
      params: { slug, page: undefined },
      props: { genre, anime, totalPages, pageNumber: 1 },
    });

    for (let page = 2; page <= totalPages; page++) {
      paths.push({
        params: { slug, page: String(page) },
        props: { genre, anime, totalPages, pageNumber: page },
      });
    }
  }

  return paths;
}

const { genre, anime, totalPages, pageNumber } = Astro.props;
const items = paginate(anime, pageNumber);
const slug = Astro.params.slug;
const isFirstPage = pageNumber === 1;

const title = isFirstPage
  ? `Anime ${genre}`
  : `Anime ${genre} — Halaman ${pageNumber}`;

const description = isFirstPage
  ? `Koleksi ${anime.length} anime genre ${genre}.`
  : `${anime.length} anime · Halaman ${pageNumber} dari ${totalPages}`;
---

<BaseLayout
  title={title}
  description={description}
  canonical={`/genre/${slug}/`}
  noindex={!isFirstPage}
>
  <div class="wrapper">

    <Hero
      title={genre}
      description={description}
    />

    {items.length > 0 ? (
      <div class="anime-grid">
        {items.map(a => <AnimeCard anime={a} />)}
      </div>
    ) : (
      <p class="empty">Belum ada anime untuk genre ini.</p>
    )}

    <Pagination

===== FILE: ./src/pages/anime/genre/index.astro =====
---
import { getAllAnime } from '~/lib/anime';
import BaseLayout from '~/layouts/BaseLayout.astro';
import Hero from '~/components/Hero.astro';
import LinkCard from '~/user-components/LinkCard.astro';
import { GENRES, URLS } from '~/constants';

const allAnime = await getAllAnime();

const genreCounts: Record<string, number> = {};
allAnime.forEach(a => {
  a.data.genre.forEach(g => {
    genreCounts[g] = (genreCounts[g] || 0) + 1;
  });
});

const genres = GENRES
  .map(name => ({ name, count: genreCounts[name] || 0 }))
  .filter(g => g.count > 0)
  .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count || a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
---

<BaseLayout
  title="Semua Genre"
  description="Jelajahi anime berdasarkan genre."
  canonical="/genre/"
>
  <div class="wrapper">

    <Hero
      title="Semua Genre"
      description={`${genres.length} genre · ${allAnime.length} anime`}
    />

    <div class="genre-grid">
      {genres.map(g => (
        <LinkCard
          href={URLS.genre(g.name)}
          variant="outline"
          padding="md"
        >
          <div class="genre-content">
            <span class="genre-name">{g.name}</span>
            <span class="genre-count">{g.count}</span>
          </div>
        </LinkCard>
      ))}
    </div>

  </div>
</BaseLayout>

     WRAPPER
     ---------------------------------------------------------- */

===== FILE: ./src/pages/anime/index.astro =====
---
import { getAllAnime } from '~/lib/anime';
import BaseLayout from '~/layouts/BaseLayout.astro';
import Hero from '~/components/Hero.astro';
import Badge from '~/user-components/Badge.astro';
import { URLS, STATUS_VARIANT, type Status } from '~/constants';

const allAnime = await getAllAnime();

const sorted = [...allAnime].sort((a, b) =>
  a.data.title.localeCompare(b.data.title, 'id-ID')
);

function getFirstLetter(title: string): string {
  const first = title[0]?.toUpperCase() ?? '';
  if (/[A-Z]/.test(first)) return first;
  return '#';
}

const letters = new Set<string>();
sorted.forEach(a => letters.add(getFirstLetter(a.data.title)));

const activeLetters = Array.from(letters).sort((a, b) => {
  if (a === '#') return 1;
  if (b === '#') return -1;
  return a.localeCompare(b);
});

const totalAnime = allAnime.length;

const groups = activeLetters.map(letter => ({
  letter,
  items: sorted.filter(a => getFirstLetter(a.data.title) === letter),
}));
---

<BaseLayout
  title="Archive Anime"
  description={`Daftar anime di Qimochi`}
  canonical="/anime/"
>
  <div class="wrapper">

    <Hero
      title="Archive Anime"
      description={`Daftar lengkap ${totalAnime} anime di Qimochi.`}
    />

    {activeLetters.length > 1 && (
      <nav class="letter-nav" aria-label="Navigasi huruf">
        <ul class="letter-list">
          {activeLetters.map(letter => (
            <li>
              <a href={`#letter-${letter}`}>{letter}</a>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </nav>
    )}

    {groups.map(({ letter, items }) => (
      <section class="letter-group" id={`letter-${letter}`}>
        <h2 class="letter-heading">{letter}</h2>

        <ol class="anime-list">
          {items.map(anime => (
            <li>
              <a href={URLS.anime(anime.id)} class="anime-link">
                <span class="anime-title">{anime.data.title}</span>

                {anime.data.status !== 'Completed' && (
                  <Badge
                    variant={STATUS_VARIANT[anime.data.status as Status]}
                    size="sm"
                  >
                    {anime.data.status}
                  </Badge>
                )}
              </a>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      </section>
    ))}

    {totalAnime === 0 && (
      <p class="empty">Belum ada anime di database.</p>
    )}
  </div>
</BaseLayout>

import BaseLayout from '~/layouts/BaseLayout.astro';

export async function getStaticPaths() {
  const { anime, totalPages } = await getStatusData('ongoing');
  return buildStatusPaths('ongoing', anime, totalPages);
}

const { anime, totalPages, pageNumber } = Astro.props;

const isFirstPage = pageNumber === 1;

  ? 'Anime Ongoing'
  : `Anime Ongoing — Halaman ${pageNumber}`;

const description = isFirstPage
  ? `Koleksi ${anime.length} anime yang sedang tayang.`
  : `${anime.length} anime · Halaman ${pageNumber} dari ${totalPages}`;

import {
  getAllEpisodePaths,
  type HydratedAnime,
  type EpisodeData,
} from '~/lib/anime';

import BaseLayout from '~/layouts/BaseLayout.astro';
import Player from '~/components/Player.astro';
import WatchTitle from '~/components/WatchTitle.astro';
import StreamSelector from '~/components/StreamSelector.astro';
import ActionBar from '~/components/ActionBar.astro';
import DownloadPanel from '~/components/DownloadPanel.astro';
import EpisodeList from '~/components/EpisodeList.astro';
import AnimeFranchise from '~/components/AnimeFranchise.astro';

export async function getStaticPaths() {
  const paths = await getAllEpisodePaths();

  return paths.map((p) => ({
    params: {
      slug: p.slug,
      number: String(p.episode.number),
    },
    props: {
      anime: p.anime,
      episode: p.episode,
      totalEpisodes: p.totalEpisodes,
    },
  }));
}

interface Props {
  anime: HydratedAnime;
  episode: EpisodeData;
  totalEpisodes: number;
}

const { anime, episode, totalEpisodes } = Astro.props as Props;
const { data } = anime;

const episodeNumbers = data.episodes
  .map((e) => e.number)
  .sort((a, b) => a - b);

const defaultStream = episode.streams?.[0];
const defaultServer = defaultStream?.servers?.[0];
const playerSrc = defaultServer?.url ?? '';

const pageTitle = `${data.title} Episode ${episode.number}`;
const pageDesc = `Nonton ${data.title} Episode ${episode.number} subtitle Indonesia.`;
const canonical = `/anime/watch/${anime.id}/episodes/${episode.number}/`;
---

<BaseLayout
  title={pageTitle}
  description={pageDesc}
  image={data.cover}
  type="video.other"
  canonical={canonical}
  noindex={true}
>
  <div class="wrapper">
    <section class="player-section">
      <Player
        src={playerSrc}
        title={`${data.title} Episode ${episode.number}`}
      />
    </section>

    <div class="content-grid">
      <div class="content-main">
        <WatchTitle
          title={data.title}
          slug={anime.id}
          episodeNumber={episode.number}
        />

        <StreamSelector streams={episode.streams} />

        <ActionBar
          slug={anime.id}
          episodeNumber={episode.number}
          totalEpisodes={totalEpisodes}
        />

        <DownloadPanel downloads={episode.downloads ?? []} />

        <AnimeFranchise
          currentSlug={anime.id}
          franchises={data.franchises}
        />
      </div>

      <aside class="content-aside">
        <EpisodeList
          slug={anime.id}
          episodes={episodeNumbers}
          currentEpisode={episode.number}
        />
      </aside>
    </div>
  </div>
</BaseLayout>

import { render } from 'astro:content';
import BaseLayout from '~/layouts/BaseLayout.astro';
import PostCard from '~/components/PostCard.astro';
import Badge from '~/user-components/Badge.astro';
import Icon from '~/user-components/Icons.astro';

  getPublishedPosts,

} from '~/lib/blog';

export async function getStaticPaths() {

    params: { slug },
  }));

const { slug } = Astro.params;
const post = await getPublishedPostBySlug(slug!);

if (!post) {
  return Astro.redirect('/404');
}

const { data } = post;
const { Content } = await render(post);

const dateText = formatDate(data.date);
const updatedText = data.updatedAt ? formatDate(data.updatedAt) : null;

const allPosts = await getPublishedPosts();
const related = getRelatedPosts(post, allPosts, 3);

          </span>

import { SITE, GENRES, PER_PAGE, URLS } from '~/constants';
import { getAllAnime, sortByRecent } from '~/lib/anime';

import BaseLayout from '~/layouts/BaseLayout.astro';
import AnimeCard from '~/components/AnimeCard.astro';
import Badge from '~/user-components/Badge.astro';
import LinkButton from '~/user-components/LinkButton.astro';
import LinkCard from '~/user-components/LinkCard.astro';
import SectionLabel from '~/components/SectionLabel.astro';

const allAnime = await getAllAnime();

const latest = sortByRecent(allAnime).slice(0, 3);

const ongoing = sortByRecent(
  allAnime.filter(a => a.data.status === 'Ongoing')
).slice(0, PER_PAGE - 1);

const completed = sortByRecent(
  allAnime.filter(a => a.data.status === 'Completed')
).slice(0, PER_PAGE - 1);

const genreCounts: Record<string, number> = {};
allAnime.forEach(a => {
  a.data.genre.forEach(g => {
    genreCounts[g] = (genreCounts[g] || 0) + 1;
  });
});
const genres = GENRES
  .map(name => ({ name, count: genreCounts[name] || 0 }))
  .filter(g => g.count > 0)
  .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count)
  .slice(0, 8);

<script>
  const track = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.hero-track');
  const dots = document.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('.hero-dot');

  if (track && dots.length > 0) {
    const slides = track.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.hero-slide');

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        entries.forEach((entry) => {
          if (entry.isIntersecting) {
            const index = Array.from(slides).indexOf(entry.target as HTMLElement);
            dots.forEach((dot, i) => {
              dot.classList.toggle('active', i === index);
            });
          }
        });
      },
      { root: track, threshold: 0.6 }
    );

    slides.forEach((slide) => observer.observe(slide));

    dots.forEach((dot) => {
      dot.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const index = Number(dot.dataset.index);
        const target = slides[index];
        if (!target) return;

        track.scrollTo({
          left: target.offsetLeft,
          behavior: 'smooth',
        });
      });
    });
  }
</script>

===== FILE: ./src/pages/rss.xml.ts =====
import rss from '@astrojs/rss';
import type { APIContext } from 'astro';
import { SITE } from '~/constants';
import { getPublishedPosts } from '~/lib/blog';

export async function GET(context: APIContext) {
  const posts = await getPublishedPosts();

  return rss({
    title: SITE.name,
    description: SITE.description,
    site: context.site!,
    items: posts.map(post => ({
      title: post.data.title,
      description: post.data.description,
      link: `/blog/${post.id}/`,
      pubDate: post.data.date,
      categories: [post.data.category, ...post.data.tags],
      author: post.data.author,
    })),
    customData: `<language>id-ID</language>`,
  });
}

<BaseLayout

<script>
  const BATCH_SIZE = 12;

  function normalize(s: string): string {
    return s
      .toLowerCase()
      .normalize('NFD')
      .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, '')
      .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
      .trim();
  }

  function levenshtein(a: string, b: string): number {
    if (a === b) return 0;
    if (a.length === 0) return b.length;
    if (b.length === 0) return a.length;

    const matrix: number[][] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= b.length; i++) matrix[i] = [i];
    for (let j = 0; j <= a.length; j++) matrix[0][j] = j;

    for (let i = 1; i <= b.length; i++) {
      for (let j = 1; j <= a.length; j++) {
        const cost = a[j - 1] === b[i - 1] ? 0 : 1;
        matrix[i][j] = Math.min(
          matrix[i - 1][j] + 1,
          matrix[i][j - 1] + 1,
          matrix[i - 1][j - 1] + cost
        );
      }
    }

    return matrix[b.length][a.length];
  }

  function scoreTitle(query: string, title: string): number {
    const q = normalize(query);
    const t = normalize(title);
    if (!q || !t) return 0;

    if (t.includes(q)) {
      const pos = t.indexOf(q);
      return 1 - (pos / t.length) * 0.3;
    }

    const words = t.split(' ');
    let best = 0;

    for (const word of words) {
      const dist = levenshtein(q, word);
      const maxLen = Math.max(q.length, word.length);
      const similarity = 1 - dist / maxLen;
      if (similarity > best) best = similarity;
    }

    if (q.length >= 3) {
      for (const word of words) {
        if (word.includes(q) || q.includes(word)) {
          best = Math.max(best, 0.85);
        }
      }
    }

    return best * 0.85;
  }

  /* ----------------------------------------------------------
     INIT
     ---------------------------------------------------------- */
  const form = document.getElementById('search-form') as HTMLFormElement;
  const input = document.getElementById('search-input') as HTMLInputElement;
  const clearBtn = document.getElementById('search-clear') as HTMLButtonElement;
  const emptyState = document.getElementById('search-empty')!;
  const status = document.getElementById('search-status')!;
  const results = document.getElementById('search-results')!;
  const loadMoreWrap = document.getElementById('load-more-wrap')!;
  const loadMoreBtn = document.getElementById('load-more-btn') as HTMLButtonElement;
  const loadMoreText = document.getElementById('load-more-text')!;

  const allCards = Array.from(
    results.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-anime-title]')
  );

  let matchedCards: HTMLElement[] = [];
  let visibleCount = BATCH_SIZE;

  const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
  const initialQuery = params.get('q') || '';
  input.value = initialQuery;

  /* ----------------------------------------------------------
     RENDER — tampilkan N card pertama, sisanya hidden
     ---------------------------------------------------------- */
  function render() {
    const visible = matchedCards.slice(0, visibleCount);
    const visibleSet = new Set(visible);

    allCards.forEach(card => {
      card.style.display = visibleSet.has(card) ? '' : 'none';
    });

    const remaining = matchedCards.length - visibleCount;
    const hasMore = remaining > 0;
    loadMoreWrap.hidden = !hasMore;
    loadMoreText.textContent = hasMore
      ? `Muat Lebih Banyak (${remaining} lagi)`
      : 'Muat Lebih Banyak';
  }

  /* ----------------------------------------------------------
     FILTER
     ---------------------------------------------------------- */
  function applyFilter(query: string) {
    const q = query.trim();

    // Empty state — belum ada query
    if (q.length === 0) {
      emptyState.hidden = false;
      status.hidden = true;
      results.hidden = true;
      loadMoreWrap.hidden = true;
      return;
    }

    // Query terlalu pendek
    if (q.length < 2) {
      emptyState.hidden = true;
      status.textContent = 'Ketik minimal 2 karakter.';
      status.hidden = false;
      results.hidden = true;
      loadMoreWrap.hidden = true;
      return;
    }

    // Filter
    const scored = allCards
      .map(card => ({
        card,
        score: scoreTitle(q, card.dataset.animeTitle || ''),
      }))
      .filter(({ score }) => score > 0.4)
      .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);

    matchedCards = scored.map(s => s.card);
    visibleCount = BATCH_SIZE;

    emptyState.hidden = true;
    results.hidden = false;

    if (matchedCards.length === 0) {
      status.textContent = `Tidak ada hasil untuk "${q}".`;
      status.hidden = false;
      results.hidden = true;
      loadMoreWrap.hidden = true;
      return;
    }

    status.textContent = `${matchedCards.length} hasil untuk "${q}"`;
    status.hidden = false;
    render();
  }

  /* ----------------------------------------------------------
     EVENTS
     ---------------------------------------------------------- */
  function updateClearBtn() {
    clearBtn.hidden = input.value.length === 0;
  }

  input.addEventListener('input', updateClearBtn);
  updateClearBtn();

  clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
    input.value = '';
    input.focus();
    updateClearBtn();
    applyFilter('');
    const url = new URL(window.location.href);
    url.searchParams.delete('q');
    window.history.pushState({}, '', url);
  });

  loadMoreBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
    visibleCount += BATCH_SIZE;
    render();
  });

  form.addEventListener('submit', e => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const q = input.value.trim();
    const url = new URL(window.location.href);
    if (q) url.searchParams.set('q', q);
    else url.searchParams.delete('q');
    window.history.pushState({}, '', url);
    applyFilter(q);
  });

  window.addEventListener('popstate', () => {
    const p = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const q = p.get('q') || '';
    input.value = q;
    updateClearBtn();
    applyFilter(q);
  });

  // Initial
  applyFilter(initialQuery);
</script>

---
interface Props {
  variant?: 'default' | 'accent' | 'gold' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'danger' | 'outline';
  size?: 'sm' | 'md';
  mark?: boolean;
}

const {
  variant = 'default',
  size = 'md',
  mark = false,
} = Astro.props;
---

<span
  class:list={[
    'badge',
    `badge-${variant}`,
    `badge-${size}`,
    { 'badge-mark': mark },
  ]}
>
  <slot />
</span>

<style>
  .badge {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--s-1);
    font-weight: var(--fw-semibold);
    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    white-space: nowrap;
    border: 1px solid transparent;
    line-height: 1;
  }

  /* ---------- SIZE ---------- */
  .badge-sm {
    padding: 0.1875rem var(--s-2);
    font-size: 0.625rem;
  }

  .badge-md {
    padding: 0.25rem var(--s-3);
    font-size: var(--fs-xs);
  }

  /* ---------- MARK ---------- */
  .badge-mark::before {
    content: '';
    display: inline-block;
    width: 0.5em;
    height: 0.5em;
    background: currentColor;
    clip-path: polygon(0 0, 100% 0, 100% 60%, 60% 100%, 0 100%);
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }

  /* ---------- VARIANT ---------- */
  .badge-default {
    background: var(--bg-surface);
    color: var(--text-secondary);
  }

  .badge-accent {
    background: var(--accent);

import Icon from './Icons.astro';

type IconName = Parameters<typeof Icon>[0]['name'];

interface Props {
  type?: 'button' | 'submit' | 'reset';
  variant?: 'accent' | 'outline' | 'ghost' | 'gold' | 'danger';
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
  icon?: IconName;
  iconPos?: 'prefix' | 'suffix';
  block?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  ariaLabel?: string;
  id?: string;
}

const {
  type = 'button',
  variant = 'accent',
  size = 'md',
  icon,
  iconPos = 'prefix',
  block = false,
  disabled = false,
  ariaLabel,
  id,
} = Astro.props;
---

<button
  type={type}
  id={id}
  class:list={[
    'btn',
    `btn-${variant}`,
    `btn-${size}`,
    { 'btn-block': block },
  ]}
  disabled={disabled}
  aria-label={ariaLabel}
>
  {icon && iconPos === 'prefix' && <Icon name={icon} />}
  <span class="btn-label"><slot /></span>
  {icon && iconPos === 'suffix' && <Icon name={icon} />}
</button>

<style>

type IconName =
  | 'play'
  | 'pause'
  | 'arrow-left'
  | 'arrow-right'
  | 'chevron-down'
  | 'download'
  | 'search'
  | 'menu'
  | 'close'
  | 'external'
  | 'star'
  | 'tv'
  | 'list'
  | 'book'
  | 'rss';

interface Props {
  name: IconName;
  size?: number;
}

const { name, size = 16 } = Astro.props;

const paths: Record<IconName, string> = {
  'play':         '<path d="M3 2l10 6-10 6V2z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none" />',
  'pause':        '<path d="M4 3v10M12 3v10" />',
  'arrow-left':   '<path d="M13 8H3M7 4L3 8l4 4" />',
  'arrow-right':  '<path d="M3 8h10M9 4l4 4-4 4" />',
  'chevron-down': '<path d="M4 6l4 4 4-4" />',
  'download':     '<path d="M8 2v8M4 7l4 4 4-4M3 14h10" />',
  'search':       '<circle cx="7" cy="7" r="4" /><path d="M14 14l-3.5-3.5" />',
  'menu':         '<path d="M2 4h12M2 8h12M2 12h12" />',
  'close':        '<path d="M3 3l10 10M13 3L3 13" />',
  'external':     '<path d="M6 4h6v6M12 4L5 11M4 6v6h6" />',
  'star':         '<path d="M8 2l1.8 4.2L14 7l-3 3 .8 4.5L8 12.4l-3.8 2.1L5 10 2 7l4.2-.8z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none" />',

===== FILE: ./src/user-components/LinkButton.astro =====
---
import Icon from './Icons.astro';

type IconName = Parameters<typeof Icon>[0]['name'];

interface Props {
  href?: string;
  variant?: 'accent' | 'outline' | 'ghost' | 'gold';
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
  icon?: IconName;
  iconPos?: 'prefix' | 'suffix';
  external?: boolean;
  block?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  hidden?: boolean;
  ariaLabel?: string;
  class?: string;
  [key: string]: any;
}

const {
  href = '#',
  variant = 'accent',
  size = 'md',
  icon,
  iconPos = 'prefix',
  external = false,
  block = false,
  disabled = false,
  hidden = false,
  ariaLabel,
  class: className,
  ...rest
} = Astro.props;

const Tag = disabled ? 'span' : 'a';
---

<Tag
  href={disabled ? undefined : href}
  class:list={[
    'link-btn',
    `btn-${variant}`,
    `btn-${size}`,
    {
      'btn-block': block,
      'link-btn--disabled': disabled,
    },
    className,
  ]}
  aria-label={ariaLabel}
  aria-disabled={disabled ? 'true' : undefined}
  hidden={hidden}
  target={!disabled && external ? '_blank' : undefined}
  rel={!disabled && external ? 'noopener noreferrer' : undefined}
  {...rest}
>
  {icon && iconPos === 'prefix' && <Icon name={icon} />}
  <span class="btn-label"><slot /></span>
  {icon && iconPos === 'suffix' && <Icon name={icon} />}
</Tag>

<style>
  .link-btn {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: var(--s-2);
    border-radius: var(--r-full);
    font-weight: var(--fw-semibold);
    letter-spacing: 0.05em;
    text-decoration: none;
    white-space: nowrap;
    border: 1px solid transparent;
    transition: all var(--t-fast);
    cursor: pointer;
    user-select: none;
  }

  .link-btn[hidden] {
    display: none;
  }

  .btn-sm { padding: var(--s-1) var(--s-3); font-size: 0.6875rem; min-height: 1.875rem; }
  .btn-md { padding: var(--s-2) var(--s-4); font-size: var(--fs-xs); min-height: 2.25rem; }
  .btn-lg { padding: var(--s-3) var(--s-5); font-size: var(--fs-sm); min-height: 2.75rem; }

  .btn-label {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
  }

  .link-btn :global(svg) {
    width: 1.1em;
    height: 1.1em;
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }

  .btn-accent {
    background: var(--accent);
    color: var(--accent-text);
    border-color: var(--accent);
  }
  .btn-accent:hover {
    background: var(--accent-hover);
    border-color: var(--accent-hover);
    color: var(--accent-text);
  }

  .btn-gold {
    background: var(--gold);
    color: #1a1a1a;
    border-color: var(--gold);
  }
  .btn-gold:hover {
    background: var(--gold-hover);
    border-color: var(--gold-hover);
    color: #1a1a1a;
  }

  .btn-outline {
    background: transparent;
    color: var(--text-primary);
    border-color: var(--border);
  }
  .btn-outline:hover {
    border-color: var(--accent);
    color: var(--accent);
  }

  .btn-ghost {
    background: transparent;
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    border-color: transparent;
  }
  .btn-ghost:hover {
    background: var(--bg-hover);
    color: var(--text-primary);
  }

  .btn-block { display: flex; width: 100%; }

  .link-btn--disabled {
    color: var(--text-disabled);
    pointer-events: none;
    cursor: not-allowed;
    opacity: 0.6;
  }

  .link-btn--disabled.btn-outline {
    border-color: var(--border);
    color: var(--text-disabled);
  }
</style>

interface Props {
  href: string;
  variant?: 'default' | 'outline' | 'flat';
  padding?: 'none' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
  external?: boolean;
  lift?: boolean;
  class?: string;
  ariaLabel?: string;
}

const {
  href,
  variant = 'default',
  padding = 'none',
  external = false,
  lift = true,
  class: className,
  ariaLabel,
} = Astro.props;
---

<a
  href={href}
  class:list={[
    'link-card',
    `card-${variant}`,
    `card-pad-${padding}`,
    { 'card-lift': lift },
    className,
  ]}
  aria-label={ariaLabel}
  target={external ? '_blank' : undefined}
  rel={external ? 'noopener noreferrer' : undefined}
>
  <slot />
</a>

<style>
  .link-card {
    display: block;

    border: 1px solid transparent;
    color: inherit;
    text-decoration: none;
    transition: transform var(--t-fast), border-color var(--t-fast), background var(--t-fast);
    overflow: hidden;
  }

  .card-default {
    background: var(--bg-elevated);
  }
  .card-default:hover {
    background: var(--bg-surface);
  }

  .card-outline {
    background: transparent;
    border-color: var(--border);
  }
  .card-outline:hover {
    border-color: var(--accent);
  }

  .card-flat {
    background: var(--bg-surface);
  }
  .card-flat:hover {
    background: var(--bg-hover);
  }

  .card-pad-none { padding: 0; }
  .card-pad-sm   { padding: var(--s-3); }
  .card-pad-md   { padding: var(--s-4); }
  .card-pad-lg   { padding: var(--s-6); }

  .card-lift:hover {
    transform: translateY(-2px);
  }
</style>
